Does an internal signature move before the behavioural gap does?

  0.00          81.5%         65.5%      5.7%        6.9%   -5.628   113.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45     100.0%      100.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      60.0%       57.1% 
       >0.35     367      94.7%       94.4% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          79.8%         43.4%     61.7%        2.8%   -1.960    48.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      10.0%       18.2% 
   0.05-0.15      45      54.2%       56.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      43.9%       41.5% 
       >0.35     367      89.6%       91.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          80.9%         40.3%     91.0%        3.2%   -0.136    17.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      26.7%       26.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      45.2%       47.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      47.5%       45.9% 
       >0.35     367      95.3%       93.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          90.7%         28.7%     95.3%        2.9%    0.152    13.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      66.7%       58.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      54.8%       57.1% 
   0.15-0.35      68      80.6%       76.2% 
       >0.35     367      98.3%       98.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          90.3%         30.3%     95.3%        3.3%    0.158    13.0

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      50.0%       53.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      59.0%       57.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      73.1%       67.7% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       99.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          90.5%         29.0%     99.6%        3.1%    0.412     8.8

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      40.0%       37.5% 
   0.05-0.15      45      56.8%       59.1% 
   0.15-0.35      68      80.6%       76.5% 
       >0.35     367      99.2%       99.2% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       50.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      59.1%       60.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      90.6%       87.9% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       99.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.3%         26.1%     99.4%        2.9%    0.409     9.1

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      57.1%       62.5% 
   0.05-0.15      45      68.2%       68.2% 
   0.15-0.35      68      89.4%       88.1% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          92.9%         27.0%     99.2%        3.1%    0.397     9.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      53.3%       55.6% 
   0.15-0.35      68      90.9%       89.4% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.5%         25.8%     99.2%        3.0%    0.399     9.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      65.9%       68.2% 
   0.15-0.35      68      92.6%       94.1% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%       99.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.7%         24.4%     99.2%        3.0%    0.396     9.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      40.0%       53.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      73.3%       73.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      92.4%       89.7% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          93.3%         27.0%     98.4%        3.0%    0.353    10.0

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      60.0%       61.4% 
   0.15-0.35      68      91.0%       88.1% 
       >0.35     367      99.5%       99.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       43.8% 
   0.05-0.15      45      64.4%       65.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      91.2%       91.2% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          95.3%         24.8%     98.6%        3.2%    0.365    10.0

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      56.2%       56.2% 
   0.05-0.15      45      74.4%       74.4% 
   0.15-0.35      68      95.6%       92.6% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          92.4%         27.8%     98.4%        3.0%    0.356     9.8

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      31.2%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      57.8%       53.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      95.3%       93.8% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.5%         26.0%     99.8%        3.1%    0.433     8.7

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       37.5% 
   0.05-0.15      45      68.9%       68.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.1%       95.6% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       37.5% 
   0.05-0.15      45      60.0%       60.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.1%       95.6% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.4%         26.0%    100.0%        3.1%    0.445     8.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       37.5% 
   0.05-0.15      45      66.7%       64.4% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.1%       97.1% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.7%         25.3%     99.6%        3.1%    0.423     8.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      31.2%       31.2% 
   0.05-0.15      45      75.6%       71.1% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%       97.1% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          95.1%         24.6%     99.4%        3.1%    0.410     9.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      56.2%       50.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      60.0%       66.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%       98.5% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          95.2%         25.2%     99.8%        2.9%    0.435     8.7

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       50.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      66.7%       68.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.1%       97.1% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.9%         25.2%     99.8%        3.1%    0.433     8.8

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       37.5% 
   0.05-0.15      45      68.9%       70.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%       97.1% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          95.9%         24.0%     99.2%        3.1%    0.404     9.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      46.7%       46.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      80.0%       80.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      95.5%       95.5% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          95.8%         24.4%    100.0%        3.1%    0.446     8.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       37.5% 
   0.05-0.15      45      80.0%       80.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      97.1%       97.1% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          93.8%         26.6%     99.8%        2.9%    0.434     8.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      62.2%       62.2% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%       97.1% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          95.3%         25.0%     99.4%        3.1%    0.412     9.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      53.3%       60.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      68.9%       68.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      95.5%       95.5% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      40.0%       46.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      73.2%       72.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68     100.0%      100.0% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.9%         25.9%     98.8%        3.2%    0.378     9.7

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      46.7%       35.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      77.8%       75.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      96.9%       93.9% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       99.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          94.7%         25.5%     98.6%        3.2%    0.364     9.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       40.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      66.7%       69.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      94.0%       95.5% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       99.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          93.3%         26.3%     99.4%        3.1%    0.408     9.1

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      43.8%       43.8% 
   0.05-0.15      45      65.9%       61.4% 
   0.15-0.35      68      91.0%       89.7% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          96.6%         23.7%     99.8%        3.1%    0.436     8.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      60.0%       62.5% 
   0.05-0.15      45      68.9%       75.6% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%      100.0% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          93.3%         27.0%     99.8%        3.1%    0.432     8.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      68.9%       66.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      94.0%       91.0% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      40.0%       40.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      77.3%       81.8% 
   0.15-0.35      68      98.5%       98.5% 
       >0.35     367      99.7%       99.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.
